## Administrative Monetary Penalties (Canada Labour Code) Regulations {#administrative-monetary-penalties-canada-labour-code-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2020-260
:::

[CANADA LABOUR CODE](/eng/acts/L-2)

Registration 2020-12-04

Administrative Monetary Penalties (Canada Labour Code) Regulations

P.C. 2020-978 2020-12-04
::::

::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Labour, pursuant to section 270[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24550){#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24550-ID0EGCAA}
of the [Canada Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24549){#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24549-ID0EDCAA},
makes the annexed [Administrative Monetary Penalties (Canada Labour
Code) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2020-260).

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24550-ID0EGCAA){#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24550}S.C.
  2017, c. 20, s. 377
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24549-ID0EDCAA){#fn_81000-2-3823_hq_24549}R.S., c.
  L-2
  :::
:::

## [Definition]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278161 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,
[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canada Labour
Code](/eng/acts/L-2).

## [Violations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278164 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designations

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** The following are
designated as violations that may be proceeded with in accordance with
Part IV of the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any provision of Part II of the
  Act, or of any Regulations made under that Part, that is specified in
  column 1 of Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any provision of Part III of the
  Act, or of any Regulations made under that Part, that is specified in
  column 1 of Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 2;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any direction issued under any
  provision of Part II of the Act that is specified in column 1 of Part
  3 of Schedule 1;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any order made under any
  provision of Part II of the Act that is specified in column 1 of Part
  4 of Schedule 1;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any order made or issued under
  any provision of Part III of the Act that is specified in column 1 of
  Part 3 of Schedule 2; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the failure to comply with any condition of a permit
  issued under section 176 of the Act, as specified in column 1 of Part
  4 of Schedule 2.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of violations

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** A violation related to any
provision specified in column 1 of a Part of Schedule 1 or 2 is
classified as a Type A, B, C, D or E violation as specified in column 2
of the respective schedule.

## [Penalties]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278175 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** The amount of the penalty
for each violation is the sum of the baseline penalty amount determined
under section 5 and the history of non-compliance amount, if any,
determined under section 6.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Baseline penalty amount

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** The baseline penalty amount
for a violation is the amount set out in column 3 of Part 2 of Schedule
3 that corresponds to the category of the person or department that is
believed to have committed the violation and the type of violation the
person or department is believed to have committed, as set out in
columns 1 and 2, respectively, of that Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}History of non-compliance amount

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  person or department that is believed to have committed the violation
  has a history of non-compliance, the history of non-compliance amount
  is equal to twice the baseline penalty amount determined under section
  5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}History of non-compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person or department has a history of
  non-compliance if, in the five years preceding the day on which the
  notice of violation is issued, the person or department

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was issued a notice of violation for a violation
    that was of the same type or of a type of a greater gravity and the
    person or department was ultimately considered, under section 289 or
    290 of the Act, to have committed the violation or there was a final
    determination, under section 284 or 287 of the Act, that the person
    or department committed the violation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} was found guilty of an offence; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} was subject to an order made under section 153 of
    the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the gravity of violation types increases in
    alphabetical order; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a violation, offence or order is taken into account
    only if it relates to the same Part of the Act -- or any regulation
    made under the same Part -- as the violation set out in the notice
    of violation.

## [Payment]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278192 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Early payment

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person
  or department named in a notice of violation for a Type A, B or C
  violation may pay an amount equal to one half of the penalty, in
  complete satisfaction of the penalty, within 20 days after the day on
  which the notice is served.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Payment is deemed to be made

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the payment is sent by regular mail, on the day
    indicated on the postmark stamped on the envelope or, if no postmark
    is legible, on the day on which the payment is received by the
    Department of Employment and Social Development;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the payment is sent by registered mail or
    courier, on the day indicated on the receipt issued by the post
    office or the courier or, in the absence of a receipt, on the day on
    which the payment is received by the Department of Employment and
    Social Development; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the payment is made electronically, on the day
    indicated by the electronic system used by the Department of
    Employment and Social Development for the purpose of receiving
    electronic payments.

## [Service]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278201 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of service

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), a notice of violation issued under subsection
  276(1) of the Act is to be served

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an individual,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by leaving a copy of it with the individual at
      any place or with someone who appears to be an adult member of the
      same household at the individual's last known address or usual
      place of residence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by sending a copy of it by registered mail or
      courier to the individual's last known address, usual place of
      residence, place of business or workplace, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by sending a copy of it by fax or other
      electronic means to the individual; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other person or a department,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by leaving a copy of it with the person's or
      department's authorized representative or with an officer or other
      individual who appears to direct or control the head office or
      place of business of the person or department or the authorized
      representative,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by sending a copy of it by registered mail or
      courier to the person's or department's authorized representative
      or to the head office or place of business of the person or
      department or the authorized representative, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by sending a copy of it by fax or other
      electronic means to an individual referred to in subparagraph (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substitutional service

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person or department cannot reasonably be served
  by any method listed in subsection (1), the person or department may
  be served by leaving a copy of the notice of violation at the person's
  or department's last known address or place of business or, in the
  case of an individual, at the individual's usual place of residence or
  workplace.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of service

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Service may be proved by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an acknowledgement of service signed by or on
    behalf of the person or department served, specifying the date and
    location of service;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certificate of service signed by the person who
    effected the service, stating that service was made on the person or
    department named in the certificate and indicating the means by
    which and day on which service was effected; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a record of electronic transmission setting out the
    date and time of transmission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date service effective

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Service is deemed to have been effected

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of service by registered mail or
    courier and in the absence of an acknowledgement of service or a
    certificate of service, on the seventh day after the day on which
    the notice is sent, as indicated on the receipt issued by the post
    office or the courier; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of service by fax or other electronic
    means, despite sections 2 and 3 of the [Electronic Documents and
    Electronic Information Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2014-117),
    on the day on which it is sent, as indicated on the record of
    electronic transmission.

## [Request for Review]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278224 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** A request for review made
under section 281 of the Act must be in writing and must contain a
statement of the grounds of review.

## [Regular Rate of Wages]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278227 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation and determination

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of section 288 of the Act, the regular rate of wages of an
  employee who is paid on a basis other than an hourly rate of wages is
  calculated or determined in accordance with this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) to (6), if the employee
  worked for at least one hour during the four-week period preceding a
  week in which the employee attends the appeal proceeding in response
  to a summons by the Board, the employee's regular rate of wages for
  that week is calculated by dividing the wages that the employee earned
  in that period by the hours that they worked during the same period,
  excluding overtime hours.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative four-week period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) to (6), if the employee
  did not work for at least one hour in the period referred to in
  subsection (2) but did work for at least one hour in the preceding
  four-week period, their regular rate of wages is calculated using the
  formula set out in subsection (2) but in respect of the preceding
  four-week period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employee paid on commission basis

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (5) and (6), if the employee's
  wages are paid, in whole or in part, on a commission basis and the
  employee has completed at least 12 weeks of continuous employment for
  their employer, the employee's regular rate of wages for the week in
  which the employee attends the appeal proceeding in response to a
  summons by the Board is calculated by dividing the amount of wages
  that they earned in the 12-week period preceding that week by the
  number of hours that the employee worked during that period, excluding
  overtime hours.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Collective agreement

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), if a collective agreement
  that is binding on the employee and employer sets out a regular rate
  of wages that is applicable to the employee, or a method for
  calculating it, that rate, or the rate calculated following that
  method, is the employee's regular rate of wages.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum wage

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the following circumstances, an employee's regular
  rate of wages is the minimum wage rate referred to in Part III of the
  Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the employee's regular rate of wages cannot be
    calculated or determined in accordance with any of subsections (2)
    to (5), because the employer is not required, under paragraph
    24(2)(d) of the [Canada Labour Standards
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._986), to keep records of
    hours worked by the employee each day and the employer cannot
    otherwise determine the number of hours that the employee worked
    during the applicable period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employee's regular rate of wages, as calculated
    or determined in accordance with any of subsections (2) to (5), is
    less than that minimum wage rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded amounts

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (2) to (4), vacation
  pay, general holiday pay, personal leave pay, pay for leave for
  victims of family violence, bereavement leave pay, overtime pay and
  pay received under section 146.5, subsection 205(2) or 251.12(5) or
  section 288 of the Act are not taken into account in the calculation
  of wages earned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[week]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[week]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period between midnight on
  Saturday and midnight on the immediately following Saturday.

## [Publication]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278247 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other information

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** The following information
is prescribed for the purposes of section 295 of the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the city, town or other locality and the province in
  which the employer is located;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a violation related to the
  contravention of an order or a direction, information about the
  circumstances that gave rise to the making or issuance of the order or
  the issuance of the direction, as the case may be;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether the employer has complied with the provision,
  order or direction that gave rise to the violation and, if applicable,
  the date of that compliance;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} whether the employer has paid the penalty and, if
  applicable, the date when the payment was made;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the date of the review decision; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the date of the appeal decision.

## [Transitional Provision]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278256 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type A violations

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** Designations, under
section 2, of contraventions and failures as violations that are
classified, under section 3, as Type A violations are not effective
until the first anniversary of the day on which these Regulations come
into force.

## [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1278259 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}S.C. 2017, c. 20

**[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_SOR-2020-260_e_transform_hq_28372){#fn_SOR-2020-260_e_transform_hq_28372-ID0EBCA}[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13
.sectionLabel}** These Regulations come into force on the day on which
section 377 of the [Budget Implementation Act, 2017, No.
1](/eng/acts/B-9.859) comes into force, but if they are registered after
that day, they come into force on the day on which they are registered.

- ::: Footnote

## [SCHEDULE 3]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 5)]{.OriginatingRef}[Penalty Amount]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1278389 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Definitions]{.HTitleText1} {#part-1definitions .SchedHeadL1}

- [1]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this Schedule.

  [[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person other than an
      individual. ([entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any business that is
      not a micro business or a small business. ([grande
      entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[micro business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[micro business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any business that, at
      the time a notice of violation is issued under subsection 276(1)
      of the Act, has fewer than five employees or, at the end of its
      last fiscal year before a notice of violation is issued, has less
      than \$30,000 in annual gross revenue. ([micro
      entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[small business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[small business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any business, other
      than a micro business, that, at the time a notice of violation is
      issued under subsection 276(1) of the Act, has fewer than 100
      employees or, at the end of its last fiscal year before a notice
      of violation is issued, has less than \$5 million in annual gross
      revenue. ([petite entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
